Let me give you the **cleanest, confirmed working Node.js WebSocket connection block** based on that exact Polygon WebSocket spec.

---

# 🛠️ Drop-In Working Polygon WebSocket Code (Crypto feed)

```js
const WebSocket = require('ws');

const apiKey = '0gp6oKkWwriN0WInvwu539Ch6iJAOcLK';
const socket = new WebSocket('wss://socket.polygon.io/crypto');

socket.on('open', () => {
  console.log('🟢 WebSocket connected');

  // Authenticate
  socket.send(JSON.stringify({
    action: 'auth',
    params: apiKey
  }));

  // Subscribe to BTC-USD and ETH-USD 1-second aggregate stream
  socket.send(JSON.stringify({
    action: 'subscribe',
    params: 'XAS.BTC-USD,XAS.ETH-USD'
  }));
});

socket.on('message', (data) => {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    parsed.forEach(msg => {
      if (msg.ev === 'XAS') {
        console.log(`[DATA] ${msg.sym}: Close=${msg.c}, Volume=${msg.v}, Time=${new Date(msg.e).toISOString()}`);
      } else if (msg.ev === 'status') {
        console.log(`[STATUS] ${msg.message}`);
      }
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('❌ Error parsing message:', e);
  }
});

socket.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('❌ WebSocket error:', err);
});

socket.on('close', () => {
  console.warn('⚠️ WebSocket closed. Reconnecting in 3s...');
  setTimeout(() => {
    // Optional: reinitialize here
  }, 3000);
});
```

---

### 🔄 You Can Swap to Stocks by Changing:
```js
const socket = new WebSocket('wss://socket.polygon.io/stocks');
```
And your `params` to:
```js
params: 'AM.AAPL,AM.MSFT'
```

# OGZ Prime V5.0 - "Bones Edition" [Sandbox Mode]

## Overview
OGZ Prime V5 is a real-time crypto trading sandbox bot designed for high-frequency candle analysis, trade decision-making, and greenstack growth under controlled conditions.

This version is a lightweight "bones" build intended to establish a stable operational foundation before advanced AI layering (V6 and beyond).

---

## Core Features
- Real-time Binance price feed via WebSocket (`BTCUSDT@trade`)
- Single timeframe candle tracking (1-second flow)
- Technical analysis core using:
  - Smoothed RSI (Wilder Method) [*V6 patch pending*]
  - Basic MACD
  - Trend detection (simple moving analysis)
- Basic Buy/Sell/Kill GUI buttons (optional — enable by running `node gui-server.js`)
- Full trade decision logging (`logs/trade_log_YYYY-MM-DD.txt`)
- Full PnL balance tracking (`logs/pnl_log.txt`)
- Memory-limited CandleStore (prevents memory bloat over long runs)
- Hardened WebSocket reconnection loop (auto-recover)

---

## Launch Instructions
1. Open Terminal
2. Navigate to backend folder:
3. Start backend bot:
4. Open `index.html` in your browser (drag-drop into Chrome/Edge tab)

(Optional GUI buttons):
- Open second terminal
- Start GUI server:

---

## Known Limitations
- Single timeframe only (no multi-timeframe analysis yet)
- Basic MACD smoothing (upgrade pending in V6)
- Static stop-loss (dynamic trailing upgrades pending V6+)
- Sandbox only — no real live trading connection
- No Discord/External Notifications (intentionally stripped down)

---

## Upgrade Path
- V6 will include multi-timeframe CandleBuilder (1m/5m/15m/1h)
- Advanced AI meta-decision logic
- Full smart risk management module
- Dynamic stop-loss trailing
- Discord + webhook optional integration modules
- Auto log file rotation by date

---

## Sandbox Settings
- Starting Balance: $10,000 (virtual)
- Risk Per Trade: Defined in `src/utils/config.js`
- Trade Execution Mode: Simulated (no real money)

---

## Author Notes
- Built under TB&E (The Beginning and End) philosophy
- Sandbox session 1 started: April 26, 2025
- This version officially stable and sandbox operational
- V5 bones confirmed logging, analyzing, and stacking correctly

---

## Operator Instructions
- Always check for open Node.js processes and kill them if necessary:
- Always confirm logs (`trade_log.txt`, `pnl_log.txt`) are flowing
- Watch for WebSocket disconnects (auto-reconnect now enabled)

# OGZ Prime V6.0 - Core Empire Edition [Sandbox Operator Mode]

---

## Overview

OGZ Prime V6 is a real-time crypto trading sandbox machine, enhanced with intelligent multi-timeframe analysis, dynamic risk management, partial profit optimization, and live operator dashboard visibility.

This version represents the first Operator-Grade release, capable of live adapting to changing battlefield conditions without hardcoded human intervention.

---

## Core Features

- ✅ Twin Turbo Core Brain (`OGZBrainV6.js`)
- ✅ MetaBrain Multi-Frame Voting System (`MetaBrainV6.js`)
- ✅ 1m, 5m, 15m, 1h Multi-Timeframe Candle Aggregation (`TimeframeManager.js`)
- ✅ Dynamic Trailing Stop Risk Engine (`RiskTracker.js`)
- ✅ Partial Profit Manager (Lock 50% at mid-TP) (`PartialProfitManager.js`)
- ✅ Real-time WebSocket Broadcasts (Price + Trailing Stop Live)
- ✅ Dashboard Battlefield Visualization (`final-dashboard.js`)
- ✅ Sandboxing $10k virtual balance
- ✅ Operator-grade Full Session Logging (`trade_log.txt`, `pnl_log.txt`)
- ✅ Future Expansion Ready (V6.1+, V7 SR Mapping)

---

## Launch Instructions

1. Open Terminal
2. Navigate to backend folder:
3. Start backend bot:
4. Open `index.html` in your browser (drag-drop into Chrome/Edge tab)

(Optional) GUI control server:
- Open second terminal
- Start GUI server:

---

## Dynamic Risk Management

- Smart trailing stop tied to highest price.
- Risk tightens dynamically based on RSI behavior.
- Partial profits secured automatically at halfway to TP.
- Broadcasts live trailing stop level to dashboard.

---

## Dashboard Battlefield Features

- Price chart live plotting
- Dynamic trailing stop red dashed line
- Trade log
- PnL log
- Confidence % display
- Balance tracking

---

## Known Limitations

- Sandbox mode only (no live exchange execution yet)
- Smart Resistance Detection pending (V7)
- No portfolio multi-symbol operation yet (future V7+)
- Trailing stop based on basic high price logic (advanced volatility trailing possible future)

---

## Upgrade Roadmap

| Version | Focus |
|:---|:---|
| V6.1 | Partial Exits advanced handling, PnL session summaries |
| V7 | Smart Resistance Detection, Breakout Adaptation |
| V8+ | AIML Adaptive Strategy Tuning, full Live Trade Engine options |

---

## Empire Operator Philosophy

> Built not bought.  
> Earned not given.  
> Crowned not gifted.  
> Sandboxed not simulated.  
> Greenstack protected.  
> Legacy weaponized.
